# Code stamp: 2026-10-13 21:26 UTC (date -u); HEAD db7d970 (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2's commit; the lock's sha256 0908b5b7…945e).

THE WORD (the PI's routing of 2026-10-13, chat, verbatim; curly apostrophes as typed):
"Seat’s adjudication of the G-ID-F0m fire: results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-ID-F0m_adjudication.md, sha256
f04ca5a3a6a2f11d9fba58fdf1605b349f6039993692c3620d90ba23adbed5af. Commit it by path with the close line appended to the
G-ID-F0m entry. The FAIL stands; Q-F0’s path is uncached. Go for step 13 (LOCK v0.2) on its terms and the step-10
read’s; then continue onto step 14"
Code reads "then continue onto step 14" as the PI's word for step 14, which starts after v0.2's commit. The reading is
Code's, declared in the G-ID-F0m close line (64f3c7e), and LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §8 carries it.
The terms: the adjudication's §6 item 3 (committed at 64f3c7e): "the `qual` wiring with the step-14 refusal (Q3);
uncached; the checklist line; the offline load with the snapshot in the raw's header and the entry; the raw without a
finish stamp (eight contrast sessions; the step-10 read, Q4)." The adjudication's Q3: "how the driver carries that
value (a declared constant, or a substring test on the committed lock as check (f) does for the hashes) is Code's,
declared as a PIN at the edit." The step-10 read answers for the `qual` edit at Q2, and its §2 item 5 gives the
application: "build, review, record the new sha256, launch on the PI's word; no further question".

WHAT CODE BUILT (scripts/m_sessions.py only; Code's design; seven PIN sites marked "declared at LOCK v1.0")
- The command: `qual --unit Q-F0 --proposal-file F --lock-file L --analyzer-sha256 H --package-sha256 H
  --renders-sha16 H [--resume]`.
  - It calls stages.run_gate_stage, unchanged, with gate "qual" and QUAL_COUNTS (A0 8, A0s 16; m:283).
  - Its prior is G-TIME-F0's checkpoint at the same out root.
  - The identity carries locked_path "uncached" (v0.2 §1).
  - Exit codes (the driver's, as for time): 0 PASS; 4 STOP, the stage's or any SystemExit from the loader, the stage
    or the driver's post-load checks; 2 refused before the load.
- stage_identity_extra("qual") refuses a governing lock whose committed text does not name all three of these:
  "path = uncached", the revision of record c000ac2c2057d94be3fa931000c31723aac53282 and the model id. v0.1's blob is
  refused, for one.
- stage_precheck("qual") adds two checks of its own to the step-10/12 precheck's, both made before the load:
  - the committed qual schedules;
  - the prior gate: G-TIME-F0's checkpoint, and G-ID-F0m's artifact of any verdict, not written on the stub, under
    this proposal.
- _run_stage refuses before the load, at stages in REVISION_STAGES = ("qual",), a refs/main other than the revision of
  record (the step-14 refusal; the adjudication's Q3). The refusal joins the other pre-load problems. The step-12
  post-load re-read then STOPs on any change from that read.
- time and id behave as before. The OLMo units keep their refusals; Q-F3 keeps step 19's.
- Self-test: 780 checks before the edit, 796 after.

THE RESULT
- The self-test gives 796/796 on the final driver, with HF_HUB_OFFLINE unset and with it exported as "1"
  (results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen-driver_selftest.txt).
- Mutation, each mutant in its own scratch clone:
  - round 1 (the mutation lens's batch on r0): 77 mutants; 61 killed, 16 survived;
  - round 2 (Code's batch on r1b): 86 mutants; 84 killed; the 2 survivors are the equivalents declared in round 1;
  - round 3 (Code's batch on r1e): 113 mutants; 111 killed; the same 2 equivalents;
  - final (Code's batch on r1f): 123 mutants (every mutant of every round) and 6 baselines; 121 killed; the same 2 equivalents survive; the baselines 796/796 in six conditions.
- Each round's reviewers also ran mutants of their own; their returns, below, give each one's outcome.
- The two equivalents:
  - q09_wanted_time_counts: TIME_COUNTS and QUAL_COUNTS name the same templates;
  - q11d_rev_stage_eq_qual: REVISION_STAGES is ("qual",), pinned.

  name: 'qual-driver-review-r1',
  description: 'Review the step-14 qual wiring in scripts/m_sessions.py (G-F0m-Qwen): four lenses, mutation probes, refuters',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Review', detail: 'four lenses over the step-14 driver diff' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'one adversarial refuter per lens' },
  ],
}

const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code builds and runs, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Step 14 (G-F0m-Qwen, m:283 of PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md: 24 sessions, 8 A0 contrast + 16 A0s sham, uncached) needs scripts/m_sessions.py's "qual" subcommand wired. Steps 10 and 12 wired "time" and "id" (the driver at HEAD, blob eb6aa7cf..., committed at 8da32f2; their reviews: ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-12_m-gtime-driver_review.txt and _2026-10-13_m-gid-driver_review.txt). G-ID-F0m FAILED; the seat's adjudication (committed at 64f3c7e, results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-ID-F0m_adjudication.md) confirms Q-F0 runs uncached and, at Q3, rules: "The refusal of any revision other than the one v0.2 names is built into the qual wiring at step 14 ... It compares the revision read before and after the load with the one v0.2 names ... how the driver carries that value (a declared constant, or a substring test on the committed lock as check (f) does for the hashes) is Code's, declared as a PIN at the edit." LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 is DRAFTED but not yet committed in the repository (the working tree's LOCK-m-F0.md; a copy at ${S}/lock_v02_draft.md); its §1 records path = uncached, its §4 the model id and the snapshot c000ac2c2057d94be3fa931000c31723aac53282. The patched driver (uncommitted, NOT in the repository's working tree) is ${S}/m_sessions.qual.py; the diff against HEAD is ${S}/driver_qual.diff; the patch scripts are ${S}/apply_qual.py and ${S}/apply_qual_tests.py; the words are ${S}/words.txt. What changed: qual wired through valence_core/m/stages.py run_gate_stage (UNCHANGED -- nothing under valence_core/m may change) with QUAL_COUNTS (A0 8, A0s 16), prior_ckpt = G-TIME-F0's checkpoint at the same out root, locked_path "uncached" (QUAL_LOCKED_PATH); stage_identity_extra refuses a qual run whose governing lock's committed text does not name "path = uncached", QWEN_REVISION and QWEN_MODEL_ID; stage_precheck runs the prior gate for qual (which for the Qwen qualification also requires G-ID-F0m's artifact, any verdict); _run_stage refuses before the load, at stages in REVISION_STAGES = ("qual",), a refs/main other than QWEN_REVISION; new PIN sites marked "declared at LOCK v1.0" (the step-10 read's Q2: a qual wiring's blob is recorded in v1.0); v0.2's existing PIN texts deliberately untouched (v0.2's PIN-index addendum records them at blob eb6aa7cf...). The self-test: 780 -> 787 checks, all pass. THE SELF-TEST NEEDS v0.2 COMMITTED: use ${S}/runmut14.sh, which clones, commits the v0.2 draft and its addendum in the clone, copies the patched driver, applies one mutation with your mutator script and runs the self-test under a model- and network-blocking net: usage runmut14.sh <mutator.py> <id> [o|-]; output ${S}/out/<id>.txt; ~170 s each; the baseline (00_baseline) is 787/787.
HARD RULES: Do NOT edit any file in ${REPO}. No git command that writes in ${REPO} (read-only git is fine; scratch clones are yours). Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model or run any stage on Qwen. Never open or print data/m/ckpt/* (pick-bearing). Do not write under ${REPO}/data/m. Put your own files under ${S}/<your-lens>/. Be concrete: file:line (in m_sessions.qual.py), failure scenario, consequence.`
const FINDINGS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['blocker', 'major', 'minor', 'note'] },
    where: { type: 'string' }, claim: { type: 'string' }, failure_scenario: { type: 'string' }, suggested_fix: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'claim', 'failure_scenario', 'suggested_fix'] } },
  checked_and_clean: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'checked_and_clean'] }
const VERDICTS = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string' },
}, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'correctness', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: CORRECTNESS AND BLINDING. Check the qual path end to end: argparse wiring and dispatch; stage_identity_extra for "qual" (the lock-text check -- what exactly is tested, could v0.1's blob or a partial lock pass, could the check refuse the real v0.2; _extra's locked_path validation); stage_precheck's schedules for qual, the prior gate call (stage "qual", require_real, the same out root as run_gate_stage's prior_ckpt), whether the precheck and the stage agree on the G-ID artifact's location (guard.GATES_DIR) and admissibility (stages.gid_artifact_problems: any verdict, the proposal, not on the stub); the revision refusal in _run_stage (placement before the DRIVER/SNAPSHOT lines and the load; collected with the other problems; behaviour when qwen_snapshot returns no info; the post-load MOVED check's coverage of "the revision read after the load"); the run_gate_stage call (templates_counts order and values vs the committed schedule files' n and keys.templates; prior_ckpt; out_root); exit codes for qual's statuses (PASS 0, STOP 4; can PAUSE occur on Qwen qual?); that time and id behave exactly as before (REVISION_STAGES scope). BLINDING: G-F0m-Qwen has 8 CONTRAST sessions: confirm nothing the driver adds prints a pick, a contrast free-round class or position, or contrast timing (m:263, m:271, m:318; the step-10 read's Q4: no finish stamp at stages with contrast sessions -- check the driver itself prints no wall-clock stamp).` },
  { key: 'fidelity', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: RECORD FIDELITY. Check every new or changed comment, docstring, PIN text, help string, message and self-test check name against the code and the record, sentence by sentence. Quotes must be verbatim and attributed correctly (m:283, m:358 words; the adjudication's Q1 and Q3; the step-10 read's Q2; LOCK-m-F0 v0.2's section numbers §1/§4 as drafted; stages.py's PIN at :976-980 "qualification v0.2"; stages.py:644 PRIOR_STAGE; stages.py:846-853). Is "declared at LOCK v1.0" right for the new sites? Are any v0.2 PIN texts (the seven sites the v0.2 addendum ${S}/addendum_draft.txt lists: lines :410, :440, :454, :521, :540, :627, :714 at HEAD) changed by the patch -- compare each marked block in m_sessions.qual.py with HEAD's? Are the ten v0.1 sites unchanged? Do the self-test check names claim exactly what they assert (case counts "21 cases", "24 sessions", "22", etc.)? Do the module docstring and help text describe the qual path accurately (e.g., the OLMo refusal words)? Is any of Code's own choice (the form of the revision check, REVISION_STAGES's scope) presented as the seat's ruling?` },
  { key: 'mutation', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: SELF-TEST ADEQUACY BY MUTATION PROBES. Write a mutator (precedent: ${S12}/mt3/mutate12r3.py -- each mutation a list of (old, new) replacements, every target asserted to occur exactly once, compile-checked) under ${S}/mutation/, with 30-60 mutants on the qual-specific code: QUAL_COUNTS values/order; QUAL_LOCKED_PATH; QUAL_LOCK_PATH_TEXT; QWEN_REVISION; REVISION_STAGES (dropped, widened to time/id); the lock-text check (each of the three strings dropped, the check skipped, any() for all-absent); stage_precheck's wanted for qual, the prior gate for qual (skipped, stage hard-coded "id", require_real False, the prior path); the revision refusal (dropped, compared with snap2, info None mishandled, message changes); the dispatch (gate "time", counts TIME_COUNTS, prior_ckpt omitted or another stage's, resume not passed); main's qual parser; run_qual; the self-test's own new assertions (e.g. weaken an expected string). Run each with ${S}/runmut14.sh <your mutator> <id> (5 in parallel at most; wait for each batch); also a few with o (HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1). Report every SURVIVOR: equivalent (prove it) or a self-test gap (a finding, with a verified fix: a self-test addition you show kills it and passes unmutated -- test your fix by writing an augmented driver copy and running it through the same runner with the src swapped: copy runmut14.sh and point src at your augmented copy).` },
  { key: 'ops', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: OPERATIONS AND LAUNCH READINESS. The launch will be m:267's checklist line (nohup caffeinate -is /bin/zsh -c '...' >> raw 2>&1 & disown), HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, absolute paths, the snapshot id in the raw's header, NO finish stamp (contrast sessions). Check: what the run will print and write (stdout lines per session in m:263's printed form; the report, reads, checkpoint, adjudication excerpt on a STOP); its expected duration (24 sessions: 16 sham at the measured 100-262 s, 8 contrast unknown; the 488 s planning basis) and the one-process preflight during it (guard.preflight_laptop markers; what Code must not run meanwhile); resume semantics if the run is interrupted (--resume; skip-only; the prior and artifact checks on resume; the checkpoint's identity incl. lock_sha256 = v0.2's, path uncached); what happens if LOCK-m-F0.md changes (e.g. v1.0) mid-run or before a resume (the licence by file at stage start; lock_sha256 in the identity); the real dry precheck after v0.2's commit (what it should show; run a model-free dry check of stage_identity_extra("qual")/stage_precheck("qual")/qwen_snapshot in a scratch clone with v0.2 committed and the patched driver copied, HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, the REAL hub cache read-only and data/m read via the clone's committed files -- never write data/m; report what it returns). Any failure mode that would waste the run or blind badly (e.g. a STOP that leaks a contrast pick to stdout).` },
]
const reviews = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(l.prompt + `
Return findings (severity blocker/major/minor/note) and a checked_and_clean list naming what you verified and how.`, { label: `review:${l.key}`, phase: 'Review', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (rev, l) => {
    const fs = rev && rev.findings ? rev.findings : []
    if (!fs.length) return { lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (adversarial REFUTER for the ${l.key} lens). For EACH finding below, independently re-check (read code, run probes or mutants with ${S}/runmut14.sh, under ${S}/refute_${l.key}/) and try to REFUTE it. CONFIRMED = real as stated; REFUTED = wrong (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled. Also judge the suggested fix (fix_ok: yes / no / better: ...).
FINDINGS:
${JSON.stringify(fs, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
      .then(v => ({ lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
return reviews

"""Step 14 mutation probes: apply ONE named mutation to the patched driver (m_sessions.qual.py's copy in a

t = the self-test's new assertions made FALSE (a liveness probe: the self-test must fail)."""

def sub(block, old, new):
    assert block.count(old) == 1, (old, block.count(old))
    return block.replace(old, new)

    "t10_live_stdout_count": [('len(outs) - n_outs_t >= 100', 'len(outs) - n_outs_t >= 1000')],
    "t11_live_otherart": [('"G-ID ARTIFACT UNDER ANOTHER PROPOSAL"),', '"G-ID ARTIFACT UNDER ANOTHER PROPOSAL!"),')],
    "t12_live_qpending": [('            and qpend_ok,\n', '            and not qpend_ok,\n')],
}


def apply(s: str, mid: str) -> str:
    for old, new in M[mid]:
        n = s.count(old)
        if n != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}")
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    return s


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "--list":
        print(" ".join(M))
        sys.exit(0)
    if sys.argv[1] == "--check":
        s = Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        bad = {}
        for k in M:
            try:
                t = apply(s, k)
            except SystemExit as e:
                bad[k] = str(e)
                continue
            if t == s:
                bad[k] = "NO CHANGE"
                continue
            try:
                compile(t, k, "exec")
            except SyntaxError as e:
                bad[k] = f"SyntaxError {e}"
        print(f"{len(M)} mutants; " + ("TARGET COUNTS OK, all compile, all change" if not bad else f"BAD {bad}"))
        sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
    f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
    s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    f.write_text(apply(s, mid), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied {len(M[mid])} edit(s)")
--- end mutator ---

#!/bin/zsh
# step 14 mutation runner. usage: runmut14.sh <mutator.py> <mutation-id> [o|-]
#   Clones the repository (git clone --shared, scratch only), commits the LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 draft and its addendum IN THE
#   CLONE (the qual checks need the committed lock to name the revision of record), copies the patched driver
#   (m_sessions.qual.py), applies ONE mutation with <mutator.py> <file> <id>, and runs the self-test under the round-3
#   net (model imports and sockets blocked). o = HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 exported. Output: out/<id><o>.txt (+ .diff).
set -u
S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14
R=/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab
PY=$R/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python
NET=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step12/mt3/net
mut=$1; k=$2; envv=${3:-}; [[ $envv == - ]] && envv=
src=${SRC:-$S/m_sessions.qual.py}
n=${k}${envv}
O=$S/mutation/out; mkdir -p $O $S/mutation/w
W=$S/mutation/w; cd $W || exit 9
rm -rf $W/$n
git clone -q --shared $R $n || exit 9
cd $W/$n || exit 9
ln -s $R/jlens-qwen36 jlens-qwen36
cp $S/lock_v02_draft.md LOCK-m-F0.md
cp $S/addendum_draft.txt results/raw/2026-10-13_m-lock-v0.2_pin-index-addendum.txt
git add LOCK-m-F0.md results/raw/2026-10-13_m-lock-v0.2_pin-index-addendum.txt
git -c user.name=s14 -c user.email=s14@invalid -c commit.gpgsign=false commit -q --no-verify -m "scratch: v0.2 draft" || exit 9
cp $src scripts/m_sessions.py
echo "SOURCE $(shasum -a 256 $src | cut -c1-16) mutation $k env HF_HUB_OFFLINE=${envv:+1}" > $O/$n.txt
if [[ $k != 00_baseline ]]; then
  $PY -I $mut scripts/m_sessions.py $k >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1 || { echo "MUTATE FAILED" >> $O/$n.txt; exit 8; }
fi
diff -u $src scripts/m_sessions.py > $O/$n.diff
SECONDS=0
if [[ $envv == o ]]; then
  HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=$NET $PY scripts/m_sessions.py --selftest >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1
else
  env -u HF_HUB_OFFLINE PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=$NET $PY scripts/m_sessions.py --selftest >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1
fi
echo "EXIT $?" >> $O/$n.txt
echo "SECONDS $SECONDS" >> $O/$n.txt
cd $W && rm -rf $W/$n
--- end runner ---

--- summarizer: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/mutation/summ14.py (verbatim) ---
import re, sys
from pathlib import Path
O = Path(sys.argv[1])
pats = sys.argv[2:] or ["q", "t"]
for pref in pats:
    for f in sorted(O.glob(pref + "*.txt")):
        t = f.read_text(errors="replace")
        m = re.search(r"(\d+)/(\d+) m selftest checks pass", t)
        ex = re.search(r"^EXIT (\d+)", t, re.M)
        fails = [ln.strip()[7:200] for ln in t.splitlines() if ln.strip().startswith("[FAIL]")]
        tb = "Traceback" in t
        errs = [ln for ln in t.splitlines() if re.match(r"^\w*(Error|Exception)\b", ln)]
        mf = "MUTATE FAILED" in t
        verdict = "PENDING" if not ex and not mf else ("MUTFAIL" if mf else ("SURVIVED" if (m and m.group(1) == m.group(2) and ex.group(1) == "0" and not fails) else "KILLED"))
        print(f"{f.stem:44s} {verdict:8s} {m.group(0)[:9] if m else '-':9s} exit={ex.group(1) if ex else '-'} tb={tb} nfail={len(fails)}")
        for x in fails[:3]:
            print("      FAIL:", x[:190])
        for e in errs[:2]:
            print("      ERR :", e[:200])
--- end summarizer ---

--- its drafted augmenting check: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/mutation/augment14.py (verbatim) ---
"""Build the augmented driver: m_sessions.qual.py + one self-test check (the mutation lens's proposed fix),
inserted before the time/id/qual stdout-hygiene check. usage: augment14.py <in> <out>"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ANCHOR = "        probs_t = [x for o in outs[n_outs_t:] for x in stdout_problems(o)]\n"
BLOCK = r'''        # the qual wiring's declared values and near misses (mutation lens, step 14): the constants pinned to their
        # literal values (QUAL_COUNTS and REVISION_STAGES are pinned above); a lock whose path sentence fixes the
        # cached path is refused; a refs/main one hex digit from the revision of record (its 39-hex prefix shared)
        # is refused; the lock-text check binds qual alone (time and id under v0.1's lock text reach the loader); and a refusing
        # re-read after the load STOPs qual before any checkpoint
        pins_q = (QWEN_REVISION == "c000ac2c2057d94be3fa931000c31723aac53282"
                  and QUAL_LOCK_PATH_TEXT == "path = uncached" and QUAL_LOCKED_PATH == "uncached")
        a_lc = time_qwen("qa_lock_cached", lic=[], cmd="qual", snap=qs_,
                         more=lock_as(real_lock.replace(b"path = uncached", b"path = cached")))
        near_q = {**FAKE_SNAP, "revision": QWEN_REVISION[:-1] + ("1" if QWEN_REVISION[-1] == "0" else "0")}
        a_nm = time_qwen("qa_rev_near", lic=[], cmd="qual", more=no_prior, snap=([], near_q))

        a_tv = time_qwen("qa_time_v01", lic=[], more=lock_as(v01_lock))
        a_iv = time_qwen("qa_id_v01", lic=[], cmd="id", more=no_prior + lock_as(v01_lock))
        a_rr = time_qwen("qa_pl_gone", lic=[], more=no_prior, cmd="qual", loader=q_loader(),
                         snapfn=snap_seq(qs_, (["selftest gone"], None)))
        q_ref_ok = lambda v, want: (v[0] == 2 and v[1] is None and want in v[2] and v[3] == 0 and v[4]
                                    and "DRIVER " not in v[5] and "SNAPSHOT " not in v[5])
        q_reach_ok = lambda v: v[0] == 4 and v[3] == 1 and v[4] and reached in v[2] and "REFUSED" not in v[2]
        chk("m_sessions: qual's declared values are pinned (QWEN_REVISION c000ac2c..., QUAL_LOCK_PATH_TEXT "
            "'path = uncached', QUAL_LOCKED_PATH 'uncached'); a lock fixing 'path = cached' is refused; a refs/main one "
            "hex digit from the revision of record is refused; time and id "
            "under v0.1's lock text reach the loader (the lock-text check binds qual alone); a refusing re-read after "
            "the load STOPs qual ('STOP: SNAPSHOT RE-READ REFUSED') before any checkpoint, with no re-read line",
            pins_q
            and q_ref_ok(a_lc, f"its committed text does not name ['{QUAL_LOCK_PATH_TEXT}']")
            and q_ref_ok(a_nm, f"snapshot: refs/main is {near_q['revision']}, not the revision of record "
                               f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)")
            and q_reach_ok(a_tv) and q_reach_ok(a_iv)
            and a_rr[0] == 4 and f"STOP: SNAPSHOT RE-READ REFUSED ({qname})" in a_rr[2] and no_q_out("qa_pl_gone")
            and "SNAPSHOT re-read" not in a_rr[5],
            (pins_q, a_lc[0], a_lc[2][-120:], a_nm[0], a_nm[2][-120:], a_tv[0], a_tv[2][-100:],
             a_iv[0], a_iv[2][-100:], a_rr[0], a_rr[2][-100:]))

'''

src, dst = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
s = src.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
assert s.count(ANCHOR) == 1
s = s.replace(ANCHOR, BLOCK + ANCHOR)
compile(s, str(dst), "exec")
dst.write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"augmented: {dst} (+{BLOCK.count(chr(10))} lines)")
--- end its drafted augmenting check ---

export const meta = {
  name: 'qual-driver-review-r2',
  description: 'Review round 2 of the step-14 qual wiring (r1b build): four lenses over the revised driver, one refuter each',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Review', detail: 'correctness/blinding, fidelity, ops, mutation adequacy over the r1b build' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'one adversarial refuter per lens' },
  ],
}
const S = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14'
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code builds and runs, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Step 14 (G-F0m-Qwen, m:283 of PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md: 24 sessions, 8 A0 contrast + 16 A0s sham, uncached, on Qwen) needs scripts/m_sessions.py's "qual" subcommand wired. Steps 10 and 12 wired "time" and "id" (the driver at HEAD, blob eb6aa7cf..., committed at 8da32f2; reviews: ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-12_m-gtime-driver_review.txt and ${REPO}/results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gid-driver_review.txt). The words and the record: ${S}/words2.txt (READ IT FIRST: LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 is now COMMITTED at db7d970; the adjudication's Q3 sets the revision refusal; the step-10 read's Q2/Q4/Q7). This is REVIEW ROUND 2. Round 1 reviewed an earlier build (${S}/m_sessions.qual_r1.py's predecessor); its findings and their verdicts are in ${S}/r1_findings.txt and Code's dispositions in ${S}/r1_dispositions.txt. THE BUILD UNDER REVIEW is ${S}/m_sessions.qual_r1b.py (uncommitted; NOT in the repository's working tree); its diff against HEAD's driver is ${S}/driver_qual_r1b.diff and against the round-1 build ${S}/r1_to_r1b.diff; it is generated by applying ${S}/apply_qual.py then ${S}/apply_qual_tests.py to HEAD's driver (${S}/m_sessions.head.py). Self-test baseline: 790/790 with HF_HUB_OFFLINE unset and =1. To run the self-test (or a mutant of it) use ${S}/runst14.sh <src.py> <run-id> [o|-] [mutator.py mutation-id] with OUTDIR and WDIR exported to directories under your own lens dir (it clones HEAD with v0.2 committed, copies src over scripts/m_sessions.py, applies at most one mutation, runs the self-test under a model- and network-blocking net; ~190 s each). A round-2 mutation batch by Code is RUNNING in ${S}/r2mut (86 mutants: the 77 of round 1 adapted, ${S}/r2mut/mutate14b.py, and 9 new ones, ${S}/r2mut/mut_r2.py) -- do not touch ${S}/r2mut or its processes, and run at most 2 self-tests of your own at a time.
HARD RULES: Do NOT edit any file in ${REPO}. No git command that writes in ${REPO} (read-only git is fine; scratch clones are yours). Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model or run any stage on Qwen. Never open, cat, grep or print data/m/ckpt/* (pick-bearing) in the repository or in any clone (the driver's own programmatic reads during a precheck are allowed; print nothing from them beyond the precheck's own refusal strings). Do not write under ${REPO}/data/m. Put your own files under ${S}/r2_<your-lens>/. Be concrete: file:line in m_sessions.qual_r1b.py, a failure scenario, the consequence.`
const FINDINGS = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['blocker', 'major', 'minor', 'note'] },
    where: { type: 'string' }, claim: { type: 'string' }, failure_scenario: { type: 'string' }, suggested_fix: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'claim', 'failure_scenario', 'suggested_fix'] } },
  checked_and_clean: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'checked_and_clean'] }
const VERDICTS = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, reason: { type: 'string' }, fix_ok: { type: 'string' },
}, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'reason', 'fix_ok'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'correctness', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: CORRECTNESS AND BLINDING (ids C2-n). (1) Re-check the whole qual path end to end in r1b as round 1's correctness lens did (argparse wiring and dispatch; stage_identity_extra("qual")'s lock-text check against the COMMITTED v0.2 -- it must pass on HEAD's LOCK-m-F0.md and refuse v0.1's blob; stage_precheck's schedules, prior gate and G-ID artifact handling; the revision refusal's placement and its interaction with qwen_snapshot; the post-load re-read; run_gate_stage's arguments; exit codes; time and id unchanged in behaviour). (2) Verify each round-1 disposition in ${S}/r1_dispositions.txt that touched code or the self-test was applied as stated and is correct: CB-1's line-form whitelist (is capture()'s stderr tee behaviour-preserving for every existing check -- ordering, exceptions, nested redirects; are the forms tight enough that a finish stamp or a duration on any stdout/stderr line of a wiring run fails, yet loose enough that no legitimate line can fail on a real run's variation; is the PROVENANCE exemption narrow); CB-3's new PIN text in the qual dispatch branch (is every claim in it true of the code); CB-6a's prior assertion; the new mutation-kill cases (truncated and upper-case revisions, the load_committed spy, the --help check). (3) BLINDING: G-F0m-Qwen has 8 CONTRAST sessions; confirm nothing the driver prints or writes (stdout, stderr, files outside the stage's own) carries a pick, a contrast free-round class or position, or contrast timing (m:263, m:271, m:318; the step-10 read's Q4).` },
  { key: 'fidelity', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: RECORD FIDELITY (ids F2-n). Check every new or changed comment, docstring, PIN text, help string, message and self-test check name in r1b (driver_qual_r1b.diff) against the code and the record, sentence by sentence: quotes verbatim and correctly attributed (m:283, m:358; the adjudication's Q1/Q3 and §6 item 3; the step-10 read's Q2/Q4/Q5; LOCK-m-F0 v0.2's section numbers as COMMITTED -- read HEAD's LOCK-m-F0.md lines 1-377; stages.py line citations at HEAD). Check that each round-1 RF disposition in r1_dispositions.txt is applied as stated. Do the self-test check names claim exactly what they assert (case counts, "22", "24 sessions", the new checks' names)? Is any Code choice presented as the seat's ruling? THE PIN INDEX: apply the v0.1 index's extraction rule exactly (the logic in /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step13/gen_addendum.py's blocks(): a comment-marker block runs while lines start with '#' and carry no 'PIN (Code'; a docstring-marker block runs to a blank line, a line containing 'PIN (Code' or a line starting with closing quotes) to r1b and to HEAD's driver, and report precisely which of the seven v0.2 sites (markers 'PIN (Code, declared at LOCK v0.2') and the ten v0.1 sites ('declared at LOCK v0.1') have an indexed block that differs between HEAD and r1b, and list every new 'declared at LOCK v1.0' site with its line and its block. Compare with RF-1's disposition (P315, P317, P318 changed; P314, P316, P319, P320 and the v0.1 sites identical) and report any difference.` },
  { key: 'ops', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: OPERATIONS AND LAUNCH READINESS (ids O2-n). The launch will be m:267's checklist line, as round 1's OPS-7 gives it (nohup caffeinate -is /bin/zsh -c 'cd ${REPO} && export HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 && <venv python> <abs>/scripts/m_sessions.py qual --unit Q-F0 --proposal-file PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md --lock-file LOCK-m-F0.md --analyzer-sha256 66fb5214...7d5e --package-sha256 5f646db6...d771 --renders-sha16 a04f67b0e5105a05' >> <raw> 2>&1 & disown), NO finish stamp. (1) In a scratch clone of HEAD (v0.2 committed) with r1b copied over scripts/m_sessions.py, HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 and the REAL hub cache read-only, run model-free: stage_identity_extra("qual", ...) with the real flags above (get the full analyzer and package hashes from HEAD's LOCK-m-F0.md text, which names them in full), qwen_snapshot(), and stage_precheck("qual", extra, resume_run=False, out_root=<the clone's data/m>) -- the precheck reads the clone's committed G-TIME checkpoint and G-ID artifact programmatically; print only its returned problem strings, never checkpoint content. Expected: identity accepted; snapshot at c000ac2c...; the precheck's only problems from the one-process preflight (other python processes are running) and from the clone's driver not being committed (driver_problems is not in the precheck). Report exactly what it returns. (2) Re-check round 1's OPS-1..8 dispositions in r1_dispositions.txt for completeness: anything the launch raw's header or the entry must still carry; the wait method (a background until-loop on pgrep that emits one line at the end; no Monitor streaming); resume; what Code must not run meanwhile. (3) Anything in r1b's changes that affects the real run (the capture() tee is self-test-only -- confirm; the new PIN comment; STEP_WORDS note).` },
  { key: 'mutation', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: SELF-TEST ADEQUACY (ids M2-n). Code's round-2 batch (86 mutants listed in ${S}/r2mut/jobs.txt; mutators ${S}/r2mut/mutate14b.py and ${S}/r2mut/mut_r2.py) already covers round 1's 77 mutants and nine new ones on the round-1 fixes (n01 finish stamp, n02 duration line, n03 duration in STOP, n04 epoch line, n05 stamp in the DRIVER line, n06 clock in a REFUSED line, n07 prior at DEFAULT_OUT, n08/n09 the capture tee's liveness). Do NOT re-run those. Find what they miss: read the qual-specific code and the self-test's qual checks in r1b and propose 10-20 NEW mutants that the batch does not contain and that a reasonable regression could produce (e.g. in the whitelist's forms, the spy, the revision refusal's message or ordering, the help check, the prior assertion, the dispatch PIN's claims made false in code, stage_identity_extra for qual with locked_path, the post-load re-read for qual), each with an argued expectation. Write them as a mutator under ${S}/r2_mutation/ (each mutation a list of (old, new) replacements, every target asserted to occur exactly once, compile-checked) and run them with ${S}/runst14.sh ${S}/m_sessions.qual_r1b.py <id> - <your mutator> <id> (OUTDIR/WDIR under ${S}/r2_mutation/; at most 2 at a time). Report every SURVIVOR: equivalent (prove it) or a self-test gap (a finding, with a verified fix -- an augmented copy of r1b that kills it and passes unmutated, run through the same runner).` },
]
const reviews = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(l.prompt + `
Return findings (severity blocker/major/minor/note) and a checked_and_clean list naming what you verified and how.`, { label: `review:${l.key}`, phase: 'Review', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (rev, l) => {
    const fs = rev && rev.findings ? rev.findings : []
    if (!fs.length) return { lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (adversarial REFUTER for the round-2 ${l.key} lens). For EACH finding below, independently re-check (read code, run probes or mutants with ${S}/runst14.sh, your files under ${S}/r2_refute_${l.key}/) and try to REFUTE it. CONFIRMED = real as stated; REFUTED = wrong (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled. Also judge the suggested fix (fix_ok: yes / no / better: ...).
FINDINGS:
${JSON.stringify(fs, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
      .then(v => ({ lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
return reviews

2. CODE'S ROUND-2 MUTATION BATCH on r1b (86 mutants + 2 baselines; runner, job script, mutators, job list verbatim; mut_r2.py as written now, which also holds n10 and n12, added for round 3)

--- runner: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/runst14.sh (verbatim) ---
#!/bin/zsh
# step 14 self-test / mutation runner after LOCK-m-F0 v0.2's commit (db7d970). usage:
#   runst14.sh <src.py> <run-id> [o|-] [mutator.py mutation-id]
# Clones the repository at HEAD (git clone --shared, scratch only; v0.2 is committed there, so nothing is committed in
# the clone), copies <src.py> over scripts/m_sessions.py, applies at most ONE mutation, and runs the self-test under the
# round-3 net (model imports and sockets blocked). o = HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 exported, else unset. Output: <outdir>/<id>.txt
set -u
S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14
R=/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab
PY=$R/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python
NET=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step12/mt3/net
src=$1; k=$2; envv=${3:-}; [[ $envv == - ]] && envv=
mut=${4:-}; mid=${5:-}
O=${OUTDIR:-$S/out2}; W=${WDIR:-$S/w2}; mkdir -p $O $W
n=${k}${envv}
cd $W || exit 9
rm -rf $W/$n
git clone -q --shared $R $n || exit 9
cd $W/$n || exit 9
ln -s $R/jlens-qwen36 jlens-qwen36
if [[ ${RMCKPT:-0} == 1 ]]; then rm -f $W/$n/data/m/ckpt/*.json; RMNOTE="RMCKPT: the clone's data/m/ckpt/*.json removed unread"; else RMNOTE=""; fi
cp $src scripts/m_sessions.py
echo "SOURCE $(shasum -a 256 $src | cut -c1-16) HEAD $(git rev-parse --short HEAD) run $k mutation ${mid:--} env HF_HUB_OFFLINE=${envv:+1} $RMNOTE" > $O/$n.txt
if [[ -n $mut ]]; then
  $PY -I $mut scripts/m_sessions.py $mid >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1 || { echo "MUTATE FAILED" >> $O/$n.txt; exit 8; }
fi
diff -u $src scripts/m_sessions.py > $O/$n.diff
SECONDS=0
if [[ $envv == o ]]; then
  HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=$NET $PY scripts/m_sessions.py --selftest >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1
else
  env -u HF_HUB_OFFLINE PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=$NET $PY scripts/m_sessions.py --selftest >> $O/$n.txt 2>&1
fi
echo "EXIT $?" >> $O/$n.txt
echo "SECONDS $SECONDS" >> $O/$n.txt
cd $W && rm -rf $W/$n
--- end runner ---

def sub(block, old, new):
    assert block.count(old) == 1, (old, block.count(old))
    return block.replace(old, new)

def apply(s: str, mid: str) -> str:
    for old, new in M[mid]:
        n = s.count(old)
        if n != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}")
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    return s


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "--list":
        print(" ".join(M))
        sys.exit(0)
    if sys.argv[1] == "--check":
        s = Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        bad = {}
        for k in M:
            try:
                t = apply(s, k)
            except SystemExit as e:
                bad[k] = str(e)
                continue
            if t == s:
                bad[k] = "NO CHANGE"
                continue
            try:
                compile(t, k, "exec")
            except SyntaxError as e:
                bad[k] = f"SyntaxError {e}"
        print(f"{len(M)} mutants; " + ("TARGET COUNTS OK, all compile, all change" if not bad else f"BAD {bad}"))
        sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
    f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
    s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    f.write_text(apply(s, mid), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied {len(M[mid])} edit(s)")
--- end mutator (round 1's 77, anchors adapted) ---

"""Round-2 mutator (Code): new mutants against the round-1 fixes. usage: python -I mut_r2.py FILE ID (edits in place)."""
import sys
p, k = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()


def rep(a, b, count=1):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != count:
        sys.exit(f"{k}: count {n} != {count}: {a[:120]!r}")
    s = s.replace(a, b)


END_A = '''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]'''
T0_A = '''    name = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, stage)
    extra, probs = stage_identity_extra(stage, proposal_file'''
T0_B = '''    name = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, stage)
    _t0_r2 = __import__("time").monotonic()
    extra, probs = stage_identity_extra(stage, proposal_file'''
if k == "n01_finish_stamp":          # round 1's cb14: an ISO finish stamp on stdout after the stage
    rep(END_A, END_A.replace('    return stages.EXIT_CODES', '    import datetime as _dt\n'
        '    print(f"FINISHED {_dt.datetime.now(_dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()}", flush=True)\n'
        '    return stages.EXIT_CODES'))
elif k == "n02_stage_wall_dur":      # round 1's rc1_fix_dur: a duration line on stdout after the stage
    rep(T0_A, T0_B)
    rep(END_A, END_A.replace('    return stages.EXIT_CODES',
        '    print(f"STAGE WALL {__import__(\'time\').monotonic() - _t0_r2:.1f} s", flush=True)\n'
        '    return stages.EXIT_CODES'))
elif k == "n03_stop_elapsed":        # a duration inside a STOP line (stderr, a free-text form)
    rep(T0_A, T0_B)
    rep(END_A, END_A.replace('print(f"STOP: {e}"', 'print(f"STOP: {e} (after {__import__(\'time\').monotonic() - _t0_r2:.1f}s)"'))
elif k == "n04_epoch_line":          # an epoch on a new stdout line
    rep(END_A, END_A.replace('    return stages.EXIT_CODES',
        '    print(f"END {int(__import__(\'time\').time())}", flush=True)\n    return stages.EXIT_CODES'))
elif k == "n05_driver_line_stamp":   # an ISO stamp appended to the DRIVER line (a tight form)
    rep('''            print(f"DRIVER {DRIVER_REL} {dsha}: committed at HEAD {stages._git_head()}, working tree "''',
        '''            print(f"DRIVER {DRIVER_REL} {dsha}: at {__import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()} committed at HEAD {stages._git_head()}, working tree "''')
elif k == "n06_refused_clock":       # a clock time inside the precheck's REFUSED line (stderr)
    rep('''                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)''',
        '''                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded at {__import__('time').strftime('%H:%M:%S')}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)''')
elif k == "n07_prior_default":       # round 1's rc6: qual's prior read at stages.DEFAULT_OUT (run with RMCKPT=1)
    rep('''identity_extra=extra, substrate="qwen", out_root=root,
                                        prior_ckpt=stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", root)["ckpt"])''',
        '''identity_extra=extra, substrate="qwen", out_root=root,
                                        prior_ckpt=stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", stages.DEFAULT_OUT)["ckpt"])''')
    rep('''        qname = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, "qual")
''', '''        qname = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, "qual")
        import shutil as _sh_r2
        _dq_r2 = Path(stages.DEFAULT_OUT) / "ckpt"
        _dq_r2.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        _sh_r2.copyfile(froot / "ckpt" / "Q-F0_time.json", _dq_r2 / "Q-F0_time.json")
''')
elif k == "n08_capture_no_errs":     # the self-test: errs no longer kept beside outs
    rep('''        errs.append(ebuf.getvalue())
''', '')
elif k == "n09_capture_no_passthrough":   # the self-test: captured stderr not passed on to the caller's stderr
    rep('''            sys.stderr.write(ebuf.getvalue())
''', '''            pass
''')
elif k == "n10_no_warn_filter":      # round 2's C2-5 (b): capture's ResourceWarning filter dropped (run under PYTHONWARNINGS=always)
    rep('''                warnings.simplefilter("ignore", ResourceWarning)
''', '''                pass
''')
elif k == "n12_rev_skipped_with_ckpt":  # the revision check skipped whenever a G-F0m-Qwen checkpoint is on disk
    rep('''            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:''',
        '''            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION and not stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, stage, root)["ckpt"].exists():''')
else:
    sys.exit(f"unknown mutation {k}")
compile(s, p, "exec")
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
print(f"MUTATED {k}")

export const meta = {
  name: 'qual-driver-review-r3',
  description: 'Review round 3 of the step-14 qual wiring (r1e build): fidelity, self-test correctness/adequacy, launch ops; one refuter each',

    { title: 'Review', detail: 'three lenses over the r1b->r1e changes and the launch procedure' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'one adversarial refuter per lens' },
  ],
}
const S = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14'
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code builds and runs, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Step 14 (G-F0m-Qwen, m:283: 24 sessions, 8 A0 contrast + 16 A0s sham, uncached, on Qwen) needs scripts/m_sessions.py's "qual" subcommand wired. Read ${S}/words2.txt first (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 is COMMITTED at db7d970; the adjudication's Q3 sets the revision refusal; the step-10 read's Q2/Q4/Q7). This is REVIEW ROUND 3. Rounds 1 and 2: findings ${S}/r1_findings.txt and the round-2 workflow journal summarized in Code's dispositions ${S}/r1_dispositions.txt and ${S}/r2_dispositions.txt (read both: they say what changed and why). THE BUILD UNDER REVIEW is ${S}/m_sessions.qual_r1e.py (uncommitted; NOT in the repository's working tree), generated by ${S}/apply_qual.py then ${S}/apply_qual_tests.py on HEAD's driver (${S}/m_sessions.head.py). Diffs: against HEAD ${S}/driver_qual_r1e.diff; against round 2's build ${S}/r1b_to_r1e.diff (THIS IS YOUR MAIN TARGET). The PIN-index view of r1e against HEAD by the index's extraction rule: ${S}/pin_diff14_r1e.txt (script ${S}/pin_diff14.py). Self-test baseline 795/795 (HF_HUB_OFFLINE unset and =1, PYTHONWARNINGS=always, TMPDIR under a '/7h/' segment). To run the self-test or a mutant: ${S}/runst14.sh <src.py> <run-id> [o|-] [mutator.py mutation-id] with OUTDIR and WDIR exported to directories under your own lens dir (~200 s each). Code's round-3 mutation batch (113 mutants, ${S}/r3mut/jobs.txt) is RUNNING -- do not touch ${S}/r3mut or its processes; run at most 2 self-tests of your own at a time.
HARD RULES: Do NOT edit any file in ${REPO}. No git command that writes in ${REPO} (read-only git is fine; scratch clones are yours). Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model or run any stage on Qwen. Never open, cat, grep or print data/m/ckpt/* (pick-bearing), in the repository or any clone. Do not write under ${REPO}/data/m. Put your own files under ${S}/r3_<your-lens>/. Be concrete: file:line in m_sessions.qual_r1e.py (or gen_header14.py), a failure scenario, the consequence. Report only real defects or record errors; say so plainly if you find none.`

const LENSES = [
  { key: 'fidelity', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: RECORD FIDELITY (ids F3-n). Check every comment, docstring, PIN text, help string and self-test check name that changed between r1b and r1e (r1b_to_r1e.diff), sentence by sentence, against the code and the record: quotes verbatim (LOCK-m-F0.md at HEAD, lines 1-377 for v0.2; the adjudication; the step-10 read; stages.py at HEAD with the line numbers cited, e.g. :1086-1094); attributions (the PI's word "as Code reads it"; Code's choices marked as Code's); each check name claims exactly what its assertion tests (counts, forms, "in order and byte for byte", "two recorded sessions", "PRIOR STAGE ABSENT"). Confirm each round-2 disposition that touched text was applied as stated. Check the PIN-index view (pin_diff14_r1e.txt): the two new v1.0 sites (F2-5's amended :452 block, F2-6's new :463 block) -- does either grow a v0.2 or v0.1 indexed block, or absorb other comment lines? Is the module docstring's --selftest list accurate now?` },
  { key: 'selftest', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: SELF-TEST CORRECTNESS AND ADEQUACY OF THE ROUND-2 CHECKS (ids S3-n). Read the new or changed checks in r1e: capture()'s ResourceWarning filter; the line-form check's widened clock_rx/dur_rx and path stripping; the per-run PROVENANCE check; the _provenance_line spy (installed right after n_outs_t, closed at the check -- is everything between those points a wiring run; can the ExitStack leak if an exception occurs; does any setup print a PROVENANCE line outside capture); the static AST scan of the run path; stderr hygiene over all errs; the split resume check over the interrupted run's checkpoint (q_ckpt_rec writes ck_rq1 -- is it the interrupted state, and does the precheck read anything from it?); m2_multi without the prior stub; the help phrase. For each: can it give a false PASS (show a regression it misses that a reasonable edit could produce) or a false FAIL (an environment or ordering that breaks it with no driver change)? Probe with up to 8 NEW mutants not already in ${S}/r3mut/jobs.txt (mutator under ${S}/r3_selftest/, each target asserted unique, compile-checked; run with runst14.sh, at most 2 at a time). Report survivors as findings with a verified fix, or prove them equivalent.` },
  { key: 'ops', prompt: `${COMMON}
LENS: LAUNCH PROCEDURE (ids O3-n). Review ${S}/gen_header14.py (the G-F0m-Qwen raw's header generator; it is run just before the launch, after the driver commit) and the launch procedure the dispositions describe (round 1's OPS-1..8, round 2's O2-1..6): the launch line it builds (m:267's checklist line 'nohup caffeinate -is ... & disown'; absolute paths; HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1; the flags' values -- check the analyzer and package sha256 against HEAD's LOCK-m-F0.md text, and the renders sha16 against the lock); every assertion it makes (driver committed as is; LOCK-m-F0.md is v0.2's 0908b5b7...; no commit after db7d970 touching it; no mlx-community/ at the root; qwen_mlx.py identical to HEAD); every header line's claims (true at launch time? anything a reader needs that is missing -- e.g. the snapshot dir, the checklist items, F1's audit-session condition); no finish stamp and no stamp other than the launch's in the header; the PID-wait method (kill -0 plus 'ps -p PID -o command=' containing caffeinate -- verify with a harmless probe that nohup's PID becomes caffeinate's and that the wait ends only after the zsh child has written EXIT=); the pre-launch conditions (every batch ended, pgrep, the audit session idle). Do NOT run the generator against the real raw path and do NOT launch anything on Qwen; you may run it with a raw path under ${S}/r3_ops/ to see its output (it asserts the driver is committed at HEAD -- it is not yet, so report what fails and why, or run it in a scratch clone where you commit r1e).` },
]
const reviews = await pipeline(
  LENSES,
  l => agent(l.prompt + `
Return findings (severity blocker/major/minor/note) and a checked_and_clean list naming what you verified and how.`, { label: `review:${l.key}`, phase: 'Review', schema: FINDINGS }),
  (rev, l) => {
    const fs = rev && rev.findings ? rev.findings : []
    if (!fs.length) return { lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (adversarial REFUTER for the round-3 ${l.key} lens). For EACH finding below, independently re-check (read code, run probes or mutants with ${S}/runst14.sh, your files under ${S}/r3_refute_${l.key}/) and try to REFUTE it. CONFIRMED = real as stated; REFUTED = wrong (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled. Also judge the suggested fix (fix_ok: yes / no / better: ...).
FINDINGS:
${JSON.stringify(fs, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${l.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: VERDICTS })
      .then(v => ({ lens: l.key, review: rev, verdicts: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
return reviews

--- round 2 self-test adequacy lens's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r2_mutation/mut_m2.py (verbatim) ---
"""Round-2 SELF-TEST ADEQUACY lens (ids M2-n): NEW mutants of m_sessions.qual_r1b.py, none in Code's round-2
batch (mutate14b.py's 77, mut_r2.py's n01-n09). usage: mut_m2.py <file> <id> | --list | --check <file>.
Each mutation is a list of (old, new) replacements; every target asserted to occur exactly once; --check
compile-checks every mutant and asserts it changes the file."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# --- anchors (each occurs once in r1b) ---
REVAPP = ('                sprobs = list(sprobs) + [f"refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
          '                                         f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n')
REVIF_HEAD = '            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
MAIN_RES = '                          renders_sha16=args.renders_sha16, resume_run=args.resume)\n'
ABS_BLOCK = ('        if absent:\n'
             '            return None, [f"--lock-file {TIME_LOCK_FILE}: its committed text does not name {absent} (LOCK-m-F0 "\n'
             '                          f"v0.2 §§1, 4: the path and the weights of record G-F0m-Qwen runs under)"]\n'
             '        extra["locked_path"] = QUAL_LOCKED_PATH\n')
PREFLIGHT = '    probs += [f"preflight: {p}" for p in guard.preflight_laptop()]\n'
END_A = ('        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
         '        return 4\n'
         '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]')
T0_A = ('    name = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, stage)\n'
        '    extra, probs = stage_identity_extra(stage, proposal_file')
T0_B = ('    name = stages.stage_name(TIME_UNIT, stage)\n'
        '    _t0_m2 = __import__("time").monotonic()\n'
        '    extra, probs = stage_identity_extra(stage, proposal_file')
REF_PRE = '                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
HELPQ = '            p = sub.add_parser(c, help="G-F0m-Qwen (step 14, m:283): 8 A0 and 16 A0s sessions, uncached, on "\n'
REFUSAL_ID = '        if cmd == "id":\n'
PRIOR_IF = '    if stage in ("id", "qual"):\n        prior = stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", out_root)["ckpt"]\n'


def stop_with(suffix_expr: str) -> list:
    return [(END_A, END_A.replace('print(f"STOP: {e}"', 'print(f"STOP: {e}' + suffix_expr + '"'))]


M = {
    # M2-01: the revision refusal made alone (an early return), not joined with the other pre-load problems
    # (the PIN at the refusal: "refused here, with the other pre-load problems")
    "m01_rev_refused_alone": [(REVAPP,
        '                _rp_m2 = [f"snapshot: refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
        '                          f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n'
        '                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {_rp_m2}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
        '                return 2\n')],
    # M2-02: the revision refusal skipped on --resume ("the first launch checked it")
    "m02_rev_skipped_on_resume": [(REVIF_HEAD, REVIF_HEAD.replace('if stage in REVISION_STAGES and ',
                                                                  'if stage in REVISION_STAGES and not resume_run and '))],
    # M2-03: main drops --resume for qual alone (the launch line plus --resume, OPS-4, no longer resumes)
    "m03_main_drops_resume_for_qual": [(MAIN_RES, MAIN_RES.replace('resume_run=args.resume)',
                                                                   'resume_run=args.resume and args.cmd != "qual")'))],
    # M2-04: the lock-text check moved from stage_identity_extra into stage_precheck (Qwen path only)
    "m04_lock_check_moved_to_precheck": [
        (ABS_BLOCK, '        extra["locked_path"] = QUAL_LOCKED_PATH\n'),
        (PREFLIGHT,
         '    if stage == "qual":\n'
         '        _lt_m2 = (stages._committed(extra["lock_file"])[1] or b"").decode("utf-8", "replace")\n'
         '        _ab_m2 = [x for x in (QUAL_LOCK_PATH_TEXT, QWEN_REVISION, QWEN_MODEL_ID) if x not in _lt_m2]\n'
         '        if _ab_m2:\n'
         '            probs.append(f"lock: --lock-file {TIME_LOCK_FILE}: its committed text does not name {_ab_m2} "\n'
         '                         f"(LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §§1, 4: the path and the weights of record G-F0m-Qwen runs under)")\n'
         + PREFLIGHT)],
    # M2-05..M2-10: time leaking into the free-text lines in forms clock_rx / dur_rx do not match
    "m05_stop_hhmm": stop_with(" (at {__import__('time').strftime('%H:%M')})"),
    "m06_refused_ms_epoch": [(REF_PRE, REF_PRE.replace('; nothing loaded"',
                                                       '; nothing loaded [t={int(__import__(\'time\').time() * 1000)}]"'))],
    "m07_stop_compact_stamp": stop_with(" (stamp {__import__('time').strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')})"),
    "m08_stop_compound_duration": [(T0_A, T0_B)] + [(END_A, END_A.replace(
        'print(f"STOP: {e}"',
        'print(f"STOP: {e} (after {int((__import__(\'time\').monotonic() - _t0_m2) // 60)}m'
        '{int((__import__(\'time\').monotonic() - _t0_m2) % 60):02d}s)"'))],
    "m09_stop_unitless_elapsed": [(T0_A, T0_B)] + [(END_A, END_A.replace(
        'print(f"STOP: {e}"', 'print(f"STOP: {e} (elapsed {__import__(\'time\').monotonic() - _t0_m2:.3f})"'))],
    "m10_refused_date_only": [(REF_PRE, REF_PRE.replace('; nothing loaded"',
                                                        '; nothing loaded ({__import__(\'datetime\').date.today().isoformat()})"'))],
    # M2-11: a finish stamp through PROVENANCE's exemption (a second PROVENANCE-prefixed line after the stage)
    "m11_second_provenance_line": [(END_A, END_A.replace(
        '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]',
        '    print("PROVENANCE: " + json.dumps({"finished": __import__("datetime").datetime.now('
        '__import__("datetime").timezone.utc).isoformat()}), flush=True)\n'
        '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]'))],
    # M2-12: qual's --help claims the cached path
    "m12_help_qual_cached": [(HELPQ, HELPQ.replace("sessions, uncached, on", "sessions, cached, on"))],
    # M2-13 (control; expected KILLED): qual on an OLMo unit given id's "no G-ID on OLMo" refusal
    "m13_refusal_qual_olmo_as_id": [(REFUSAL_ID, '        if cmd in ("id", "qual"):\n')],
    # M2-14: the precheck's prior gate (and so the G-ID artifact refusals) skipped on --resume
    "m14_precheck_prior_skipped_on_resume": [(PRIOR_IF, PRIOR_IF.replace('if stage in ("id", "qual"):',
                                                                         'if stage in ("id", "qual") and not resume_run:'))],
}


def apply(s: str, mid: str) -> str:
    for old, new in M[mid]:
        n = s.count(old)
        if n != 1:
            raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times: {old[:80]!r}")
        s = s.replace(old, new)
    return s


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "--list":
        print(" ".join(M))
        sys.exit(0)
    if sys.argv[1] == "--check":
        s = Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        bad = {}
        for k in M:
            try:
                t = apply(s, k)
            except SystemExit as e:
                bad[k] = str(e)
                continue
            if t == s:
                bad[k] = "NO CHANGE"
                continue
            try:
                compile(t, k, "exec")
            except SyntaxError as e:
                bad[k] = f"SyntaxError {e}"
        print(f"{len(M)} mutants; " + ("TARGET COUNTS OK, all compile, all change" if not bad else f"BAD {bad}"))
        sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)
    f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
    s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    f.write_text(apply(s, mid), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied {len(M[mid])} edit(s)")
--- end round 2 self-test adequacy lens's mutator ---

"""Round-2 correctness/blinding lens mutator. usage: python -I mut_cb2.py FILE ID (edits FILE in place).

c01_prov_finish   qual prints a PROVENANCE-shaped line carrying a UTC stamp AFTER the stage (a finish stamp in
                  the exempted form)
c02_stop_hhmm     every driver STOP line carries an HH:MM clock (no seconds, no date)
c03_stop_ms_epoch every driver STOP line carries a 13-digit millisecond epoch
c04_stderr_roster qual's STOP line carries a roster word inside its free text (stderr)
x01_aug           the self-test only: two added checks -- the roster/pick/menu check over errs, and per run at
                  most one PROVENANCE line, none after a session / BAR / STAGE line -- on the unmutated driver
x02_aug_kills     x01_aug plus c01 and c04 (do the added checks kill them?)
x03_aug2          x01_aug plus an HH:MM / 10+-digit-epoch check over every non-PROVENANCE line and a static
                  scan of the driver's run path for clock APIs, on the unmutated driver
x04_aug2_kills    x03_aug2 plus c01, c02 and c04 together (every added check should fail)
"""
import sys

p, k = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()


def rep(a, b, count=1):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != count:
        sys.exit(f"{k}: count {n} != {count}: {a[:120]!r}")
    s = s.replace(a, b)


END_A = '''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]'''

C01 = END_A.replace('    return stages.EXIT_CODES', '''    if stage == "qual":
        import datetime as _dt_cb
        print(stages._provenance_line({"record": {"event": "end"},
                                       "stamp": _dt_cb.datetime.now(_dt_cb.timezone.utc).isoformat()}), flush=True)
    return stages.EXIT_CODES''')
C04 = END_A.replace('print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)',
                    'print(f"STOP: {e}" + (f" (read {__import__(\'valence_core.m.corr\', fromlist=[\'x\']).ALL_ROSTER_WORDS[0]})" '
                    'if stage == "qual" else ""), file=sys.stderr, flush=True)')

AUG_AT = '''            not bad_w and len(lines_w) >= 500 and len(errs) == len(outs), bad_w[:4])
'''
AUG = AUG_AT + '''        probs_e_cb = [x for e_ in errs[n_outs_t:] for x in stdout_problems(e_)]
        chk("CB2-AUG: stderr of the wiring runs carries no roster label, pick line or menu text",
            not probs_e_cb, probs_e_cb[:3])
        prov_bad_cb = []
        for o_ in outs[n_outs_t:]:
            seen_cb = False
            n_prov_cb = 0
            for ln in o_.splitlines():
                if ln.startswith("PROVENANCE: "):
                    n_prov_cb += 1
                    if seen_cb:
                        prov_bad_cb.append(("after a session/BAR/STAGE line", ln[:100]))
                elif _rr._LINE_RX.fullmatch(ln) or ln.startswith(("BAR ", "STAGE ")):
                    seen_cb = True
            if n_prov_cb > 1:
                prov_bad_cb.append(("more than one", n_prov_cb))
        chk("CB2-AUG: per wiring run at most one PROVENANCE line, none after a session, BAR or STAGE line",
            not prov_bad_cb, prov_bad_cb[:3])
'''

AUG2 = AUG + """        clock2_cb = re.compile(r"\\b\\d{1,2}:\\d{2}\\b|\\b1[6-9]\\d{8,}\\b")
        clock2_bad = [(sn, ln[:120]) for sn, ln in lines_w
                      if not (sn == "stdout" and ln.startswith("PROVENANCE: ")) and clock2_cb.search(ln)]
        chk("CB2-AUG2: no wiring-run line but PROVENANCE's carries an HH:MM clock or a 10+-digit epoch",
            not clock2_bad, clock2_bad[:3])
        src_cb = Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        run_src_cb = src_cb.split("\\ndef _selftest(")[0] + src_cb.split("\\ndef run_selftest(")[1]
        api_cb = re.findall(r"\\b(?:datetime|monotonic|perf_counter|strftime|ctime|time_ns|utcnow|gmtime|localtime|"
                            r"process_time)\\b|__import__|\\bimport time\\b|\\bfrom time\\b|\\btime\\.time\\b", run_src_cb)
        chk("CB2-AUG2: the driver's run path (outside the self-test) uses no clock or timer API", not api_cb, api_cb[:5])
"""
STOP_ALL = END_A.replace('print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)',
                         'print(f"STOP: {e}" + (f" (read {__import__(\'valence_core.m.corr\', fromlist=[\'x\']).ALL_ROSTER_WORDS[0]})" '
                         'if stage == "qual" else "") + f" [{__import__(\'time\').strftime(\'%H:%M\')}]", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)')

if k == "c01_prov_finish":
    rep(END_A, C01)
elif k == "c02_stop_hhmm":
    rep('''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES''', '''        print(f"STOP: {e} [{__import__('time').strftime('%H:%M')}]", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES''')
elif k == "c03_stop_ms_epoch":
    rep('''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES''', '''        print(f"STOP: {e} (t={int(__import__('time').time() * 1000)})", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4

    rep(AUG_AT, AUG)
    rep(END_A, C01.replace('print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)',
                           'print(f"STOP: {e}" + (f" (read {__import__(\'valence_core.m.corr\', fromlist=[\'x\']).ALL_ROSTER_WORDS[0]})" '
                           'if stage == "qual" else ""), file=sys.stderr, flush=True)'))
else:
    sys.exit(f"unknown mutation {k}")
compile(s, p, "exec")
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
print(f"MUTATED {k}")

r"""Round-2 correctness REFUTER mutator (independent of r2_cb/mut_cb2.py). usage: python -I mut_rc.py FILE ID

m_all   qual prints, after the stage returns, a hand-built PROVENANCE-form line carrying a UTC finish stamp (plain
        print, not stages._provenance_line); every driver STOP line at _run_stage carries an HH:MM clock, a
        13-digit ms epoch, a unitless elapsed value and an 'XhYm' duration; qual's STOP line also carries a roster label
aug     self-test only: my own implementation of the proposed fixes, appended after the whitelist chk:
        A1 (C2-1) per wiring run <= 1 PROVENANCE line, none after a session/BAR/STAGE line, keys exactly {record,stamp}
        A2 (C2-2.1) no non-PROVENANCE line carries \b\d{1,2}:\d{2}\b or \b1[6-9]\d{8,}\b
        A3 (C2-2.2) the driver's run path (outside _selftest) names no clock/timer API
        A4 (C2-3) stdout_problems over errs of the wiring runs
        A5 (C2-5a) the whitelist re-evaluated with path tokens stripped before the clock/duration search (reports
            whether the ORIGINAL check's failures, if any, are all path-borne)
aug_all aug + m_all
aug6    aug + A6: stdout_problems over EVERY captured stderr (errs, all captures, not only the wiring runs)
aug6_all aug6 + m_all
"""
import sys

p, k = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()


def rep(a, b):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != 1:
        sys.exit(f"{k}: count {n} != 1: {a[:100]!r}")
    s = s.replace(a, b)


END = '''    except SystemExit as e:
        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]
'''
END_M = '''    except SystemExit as e:
        import time as _t_rc
        _lab_rc = ""
        if stage == "qual":
            from valence_core.m import corr as _corr_rc
            _lab_rc = " near " + sorted(_corr_rc.ALL_ROSTER_WORDS)[0]
        print(f"STOP: {e}{_lab_rc} [{_t_rc.strftime('%H:%M')}] t={int(_t_rc.time() * 1000)} elapsed={_t_rc.time() % 9999:.1f} "
              f"(1h32m)", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    if stage == "qual":
        import time as _t_rc2, json as _j_rc
        print("PROVENANCE: " + _j_rc.dumps({"record": {"end": True},
              "stamp": _t_rc2.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", _t_rc2.gmtime())}, separators=(",", ":")), flush=True)
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]
'''

AT = '''            not bad_w and len(lines_w) >= 500 and len(errs) == len(outs), bad_w[:4])
'''
AUG = AT + r'''        a1_rc = []
        for o_ in outs[n_outs_t:]:
            n_p, after = 0, False
            for ln in o_.splitlines():
                if ln.startswith("PROVENANCE"):
                    n_p += 1
                    if after:
                        a1_rc.append(("after", ln[:90]))
                    try:
                        if sorted(json.loads(ln[len("PROVENANCE: "):])) != ["record", "stamp"]:
                            a1_rc.append(("keys", ln[:90]))
                    except ValueError:
                        a1_rc.append(("json", ln[:90]))
                elif _rr._LINE_RX.fullmatch(ln) or ln.startswith(("BAR ", "STAGE ")):
                    after = True
            if n_p > 1:
                a1_rc.append(("count", n_p))
        chk("RC-A1: per wiring run at most one PROVENANCE line, {record, stamp} only, none after a session/BAR/STAGE line",
            not a1_rc, a1_rc[:3])
        rx2_rc = re.compile(r"\b\d{1,2}:\d{2}\b|\b1[6-9]\d{8,}\b")
        a2_rc = [(sn, ln[:120]) for sn, ln in lines_w
                 if not (sn == "stdout" and ln.startswith("PROVENANCE: ")) and rx2_rc.search(ln)]
        chk("RC-A2: no non-PROVENANCE wiring line carries H:MM/HH:MM or a 10+-digit 16..-19.. number", not a2_rc, a2_rc[:3])
        src_rc = Path(__file__).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        run_rc = src_rc.split("\ndef _selftest(")[0] + src_rc.split("\ndef run_selftest(")[1]
        a3_rc = re.findall(r"\b(?:datetime|monotonic|perf_counter|strftime|ctime|time_ns|utcnow|gmtime|localtime|"
                           r"process_time)\b|__import__|\bimport time\b|\bfrom time\b|\btime\.time\b", run_rc)
        chk("RC-A3: the driver's run path names no clock or timer API", not a3_rc, a3_rc[:5])
        a4_rc = [x for e_ in errs[n_outs_t:] for x in stdout_problems(e_)]
        chk("RC-A4: stderr of the wiring runs carries no roster label, pick line or menu text", not a4_rc, a4_rc[:3])
        path_rc = re.compile(r"\S*/\S*")

        def fp_rc(sn, ln):
            if sn == "stdout" and ln.startswith("PROVENANCE: {") and ln.endswith("}"):
                return None
            if sn == "stdout" and (_rr._LINE_RX.fullmatch(ln) or any(f.fullmatch(ln) for f in tight_out)):
                return "clock" if clock_rx.search(ln) else None
            if not any(f.fullmatch(ln) for f in (free_out if sn == "stdout" else free_err)):
                return "form"
            ln2 = path_rc.sub(" ", ln)
            return "dur" if clock_rx.search(ln2) or dur_rx.search(ln2) else None
        a5_rc = [(sn, w, ln[:120]) for sn, ln in lines_w for w in [fp_rc(sn, ln)] if w]
        chk(f"RC-A5: the whitelist with path tokens stripped ({len(bad_w)} original failures, "
            f"{sum(1 for x in bad_w if x[1].startswith('a clock time, an epoch'))} of them free-text)", not a5_rc,
            a5_rc[:3], [x[2][:100] for x in bad_w[:2]])
'''

        chk(f"RC-A6: stderr of all {len(errs)} captures carries no roster label, pick line or menu text", not a6_rc,
            a6_rc[:3])

    sys.exit(f"unknown {k}")
compile(s, p, "exec")
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
print(f"MUTATED {k}")
--- end round 2 correctness refuter's mutator ---

--- round 2 fidelity refuter's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r2_refute_fidelity/mut_rf.py (verbatim) ---
"""Refuter mutants for F2-4 (one per run). usage: mut_rf.py <file> <mid>"""
import sys
p, mid = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
t = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
M = {
    # a duration inside the SNAPSHOT line's \S+ snapshot-dir field (the printed line only; snapshot_line itself unchanged)
    "rf_snapdur": ('            print(snapshot_line(snap), flush=True)\n',
                   '            print(snapshot_line({**snap, "snapshot_dir": str(snap["snapshot_dir"]) + "@elapsed=4.2s"}), flush=True)\n'),
    # a millisecond epoch, a bare HH:MM clock time and an 'XhYm' duration on the INVOCATION line
    "rf_invstamp": ('    print("INVOCATION: " + " ".join([sys.executable] + shown), flush=True)\n',
                    '    print("INVOCATION: " + " ".join([sys.executable] + shown) + " t0=1760000000000 launched 13:25 took 4h12m", flush=True)\n'),
}
old, new = M[mid]
assert t.count(old) == 1, (mid, t.count(old))
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(t.replace(old, new))
print(f"MUTATED {mid}")
--- end round 2 fidelity refuter's mutator ---

--- round 2 adequacy refuter's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r2_refute_mutation/mut_ref.py (verbatim) ---
"""Refuter's own mutants (round 2, mutation lens refutation). usage: mut_ref.py <file> <id>"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
REVIF = '            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
PRIOR = '    if stage in ("id", "qual"):\n        prior = stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", out_root)["ckpt"]\n'
END = '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]\n\n\ndef run_time('
M = {
    # r02: the revision refusal skipped on a resume (condition appended at the end, not m02's form)
    "r02_rev_resume_tail": [(REVIF, REVIF.replace('!= QWEN_REVISION:', '!= QWEN_REVISION and not resume_run:'))],
    # r14b: qual alone skips the precheck's prior gate on a resume (id unchanged)
    "r14b_prior_qual_resume": [(PRIOR, PRIOR.replace('if stage in ("id", "qual"):',
                                                     'if stage == "id" or (stage == "qual" and not resume_run):'))],
    # r11b: a finish stamp re-printed in PROVENANCE's own {record, stamp} shape after the stage returns
    "r11b_provenance_reprint": [(END, '    print("PROVENANCE: " + json.dumps({"record": {"finish": True}, "stamp": '
                                      '__import__("datetime").datetime.now(__import__("datetime").timezone.utc).isoformat()}, '
                                      'sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")), flush=True)\n' + END)],
    # r11c: a {record, stamp}-shaped PROVENANCE line printed on the STOP path only (a finish stamp on a STOP)
    "r11c_provenance_on_stop": [('        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n        return 4\n    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]\n',
                                 '        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
                                 '        print("PROVENANCE: " + json.dumps({"record": {"stopped": True}, "stamp": '
                                 '__import__("datetime").datetime.now(__import__("datetime").timezone.utc).isoformat()}, '
                                 'sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")), flush=True)\n        return 4\n    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]\n')],
}
f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for old, new in M[mid]:
    n = s.count(old)
    if n != 1:
        raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times")
    s = s.replace(old, new)
compile(s, mid, "exec")
f.write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied")
--- end round 2 adequacy refuter's mutator ---
