--- round 3 fidelity lens's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r3_fidelity/mut_f3.py (verbatim) ---
"""Round-3 RECORD FIDELITY lens (F3-n): mutants that test whether two r1e check names claim what their
assertions test. usage: mut_f3.py <file> <id>. Each target asserted to occur exactly once."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

END_A = ('        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
         '        return 4\n'
         '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]')
REV_A = ('            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
         '                sprobs = list(sprobs) + [f"refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
         '                                         f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n'
         '            pre += [f"driver: {p}" for p in dprobs] + [f"snapshot: {p}" for p in sprobs]\n'
         '            if pre:\n'
         '                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
         '                return 2\n')
M = {
    # f01: a {record, stamp} PROVENANCE line, its stamp the package's clock read at the STOP, printed by the
    # DRIVER (not by the stage's _append_provenance) through stages._provenance_line -- names no clock API
    "f01_prov_line_from_driver_on_stop": [(END_A, END_A.replace(
        '        return 4\n',
        '        print(stages._provenance_line(stages._resume.append_provenance({}, {"stopped": True})), flush=True)\n'
        '        return 4\n'))],
    # f02: the revision refusal printed on its OWN stderr line ('refused: ...', a declared stderr form), not
    # joined into the REFUSED line with the other pre-load problems
    "f02_rev_refusal_own_line": [(REV_A,
        '            revp_f3 = []\n'
        '            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
        '                revp_f3 = [f"snapshot: refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
        '                           f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n'
        '            pre += [f"driver: {p}" for p in dprobs] + [f"snapshot: {p}" for p in sprobs]\n'
        '            if pre:\n'
        '                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
        '            if revp_f3:\n'
        '                print(f"refused: {revp_f3[0]}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
        '            if pre or revp_f3:\n'
        '                return 2\n')],
}
f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for old, new in M[mid]:
    n = s.count(old)
    if n != 1:
        raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times")
    s = s.replace(old, new)
compile(s, mid, "exec")
f.write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied")
--- end round 3 fidelity lens's mutator ---

--- round 3 fidelity refuter's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r3_refute_fidelity/mut_rf3.py (verbatim) ---
"""Round-3 fidelity REFUTER's own mutants (independent of r3_fidelity/mut_f3.py). usage: mut_rf3.py <file> <id>.
g01: the driver's STOP branch stamps a PROVENANCE line through the stage module's own _append_provenance (a dummy
     checkpoint) -- the F3-2 class by another route; also tests whether F3-2's suggested flag-wrapper fix kills it.
g02: the revision refusal on its own 'REFUSED: the revision of record: [...]' stderr line (not joined) -- F3-3's
     class in a REFUSED-prefixed form, so the count of 'REFUSED: <qname>:' stays one."""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

END_A = ('        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
         '        return 4\n'
         '    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]')
REV_A = ('            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
         '                sprobs = list(sprobs) + [f"refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
         '                                         f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n'
         '            pre += [f"driver: {p}" for p in dprobs] + [f"snapshot: {p}" for p in sprobs]\n'
         '            if pre:\n'
         '                print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
         '                return 2\n')
M = {
    "g01_stop_prov_via_append_provenance": [(END_A, END_A.replace(
        '        return 4\n',
        '        stages._append_provenance({}, {"stopped": True})\n'
        '        return 4\n'))],
    "g02_rev_refusal_separate_REFUSED_line": [(REV_A,
        '            revp_g = []\n'
        '            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:\n'
        '                revp_g = [f"snapshot: refs/main is {snap[\'revision\']}, not the revision of record "\n'
        '                          f"{QWEN_REVISION} (LOCK-m-F0 v0.2 §4)"]\n'
        '            pre += [f"driver: {p}" for p in dprobs] + [f"snapshot: {p}" for p in sprobs]\n'
        '            if pre or revp_g:\n'
        '                if pre:\n'
        '                    print(f"REFUSED: {name}: {pre}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)\n'
        '                if revp_g:\n'
        '                    print(f"REFUSED: the revision of record: {revp_g}; nothing loaded", file=sys.stderr,\n'
        '                          flush=True)\n'
        '                return 2\n')],
}
f, mid = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
s = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
for old, new in M[mid]:
    n = s.count(old)
    if n != 1:
        raise SystemExit(f"MUTATE {mid}: target occurs {n} times")
    s = s.replace(old, new)
compile(s, mid, "exec")
f.write_text(s, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"MUTATE {mid}: applied")
--- end round 3 fidelity refuter's mutator ---

--- round 3 self-test lens's mutator: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/r3_selftest/mut_s3.py (verbatim) ---
"""Round-3 self-test lens mutator (S3-n). usage: python -I mut_s3.py FILE ID (edits in place; each target asserted
unique; the result compile-checked)."""
import sys

p, k = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()


def rep(a, b, count=1):
    global s
    n = s.count(a)
    if n != count:
        sys.exit(f"{k}: target count {n} != {count}: {a[:120]!r}")
    s = s.replace(a, b)


# _run_stage's STOP handler (unique: the 3-line block with the stage's exit-code return)
END_A = '''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]'''

if k == "s01_stop_relays_ckpt_prov":
    # an operator aid: a STOP line relays the stage checkpoint's last provenance entry, as JSON (the package's
    # own stamp form, YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ); names no clock API and no timing-field constant
    rep(END_A, '''        _tail = ""
        try:
            _ck = json.loads(stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, stage, root)["ckpt"].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            _tail = f" (the checkpoint's last launch: {json.dumps(_ck['provenance'][-1], sort_keys=True)})"
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, IndexError, TypeError):
            pass
        print(f"STOP: {e}{_tail}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]''')
elif k == "s02_stop_fresh_prov_entry":
    # a FINISH stamp on every STOP line: a fresh provenance entry built by the package (its utc_stamp, 'Z' form),
    # relayed as JSON; names no clock API and no timing-field constant
    rep(END_A, '''        from valence_core.m import resume as _rs
        print(f"STOP: {e} (stopped: {json.dumps(_rs.append_provenance({}, {'stopped': True}), sort_keys=True)})",
              file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]''')
elif k == "s03_prov_on_stop_append":
    # r11c's attack through the package's own builder: a PROVENANCE line for the stop (a finish stamp, keys
    # {record, stamp}) printed by stages._append_provenance on every STOP path
    rep(END_A, '''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        stages._append_provenance({}, {"stopped": True})
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]''')
elif k == "s04_prov_line_on_stop_pl":
    # the same through stages._provenance_line on a package-built entry
    rep(END_A, '''        print(f"STOP: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        print(stages._provenance_line(stages._resume.append_provenance({}, {"stopped": True})), flush=True)
        return 4
    return stages.EXIT_CODES[res["status"]]''')
elif k == "s05_rev_skipped_if_sessions":
    # the revision refusal skipped when the stage's checkpoint on disk already records sessions (the split resume
    # check's hardening: a resume over recorded sessions)
    rep('''            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION:''',
        '''            _cpq = stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, stage, root)["ckpt"]
            _has_s = _cpq.is_file() and bool(json.loads(_cpq.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("sessions"))
            if stage in REVISION_STAGES and snap is not None and snap["revision"] != QWEN_REVISION and not _has_s:''')
elif k == "s06_prior_gate_only_when_clean":
    # the precheck's prior gate made only when no earlier problem fired (m2_multi, run without the prior stub, is
    # the one case that joins a prior-gate problem to others)
    rep('''    if stage in ("id", "qual"):
        prior = stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", out_root)["ckpt"]''',
        '''    if stage in ("id", "qual") and not probs:
        prior = stages.out_paths(TIME_UNIT, "time", out_root)["ckpt"]''')
else:
    sys.exit(f"unknown mutation {k}")
compile(s, p, "exec")
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
print(f"MUTATED {k}")
--- end round 3 self-test lens's mutator ---

====================================================================================================
THE PIN-INDEX VIEW OF THE FINAL DRIVER AGAINST HEAD'S (pin_diff14.py, the v0.1 index's extraction rule, verbatim from step13/gen_addendum.py's blocks())
====================================================================================================
--- script: /private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step14/pin_diff14.py (verbatim) ---
"""Step 14: the PIN-index view of a qual build against HEAD's driver, by the v0.1 index's extraction rule
(blocks() copied verbatim from step13/gen_addendum.py). Usage: python3 -I pin_diff14.py HEAD_DRIVER BUILD"""
import sys
def blocks(text, marker):
    """(line, [marked lines]) per marker, by the v0.1 index's rules (gen_pins_lock.py, verbatim logic)."""
    lines = text.split("\n"); out = []; i = 0
    while i < len(lines):
        ln = lines[i]
        if marker in ln:
            block = [ln.strip()]; j = i + 1
            if ln.strip().startswith("#") or "#" in ln:
                while j < len(lines) and lines[j].strip().startswith("#") and "PIN (Code" not in lines[j]:
                    block.append(lines[j].strip()); j += 1
            else:
                while j < len(lines) and lines[j].strip() and "PIN (Code" not in lines[j] and not lines[j].strip().startswith(('"""', "'''")):
                    block.append(lines[j].strip()); j += 1
            out.append((i + 1, block)); i = j; continue
        i += 1
    return out
h = open(sys.argv[1], encoding="utf-8").read(); b = open(sys.argv[2], encoding="utf-8").read()
for tag, ids in (("v0.1", [f"P{n}" for n in range(256, 266)]), ("v0.2", [f"P{n}" for n in range(314, 321)])):
    M = f"PIN (Code, declared at LOCK {tag}"
    hb, bb = blocks(h, M), blocks(b, M)
    assert len(hb) == len(bb) == len(ids), (tag, len(hb), len(bb))
    # v0.2 numbering: P314-P318 are the five amended sites, P319-P320 the two new at step 12 (the addendum's order)
    order = list(range(len(ids)))
    if tag == "v0.2":
        lines_h = [l for l, _ in hb]   # HEAD lines: 410, 440, 454, 521, 540, 627, 714
        amap = {410: "P314", 454: "P315", 521: "P316", 627: "P317", 714: "P318", 440: "P319", 540: "P320"}
        names = [amap[l] for l in lines_h]
    else:
        names = ids
    for nm, (lh, bh), (lb, bbk) in zip(names, hb, bb):
        same = bh == bbk
        print(f"{tag} {nm}: HEAD :{lh} -> build :{lb}  {'identical' if same else 'CHANGED'}")
        if not same:
            import difflib
            for d in difflib.unified_diff(bh, bbk, lineterm="", n=0):
                if d.startswith(("---", "+++", "@@")): continue
                print("      " + d[:150])
M = "PIN (Code, declared at LOCK v1.0"
nb = blocks(b, M)
print(f"\nv1.0 sites in the build: {len(nb)} (HEAD has {len(blocks(h, M))})")
for l, bk in nb:
    print(f"  :{l}  " + bk[0][:110])
    for x in bk[1:]:
        print("        " + x[:140])
--- end script ---

apply_qual_tests.py; the build is m_sessions.qual_r1b.py.

Correctness and blinding (C2): every one CONFIRMED
- C2-1 (= M2-3). Applied, with the refuter's keys check and M2-3's output lines. Per run (one capture, one invocation)
  there is at most one PROVENANCE line. Its JSON keys are exactly {record, stamp}. It comes before any session, BAR,
  STAGE or output-path line. The domain must hold at least 20 PROVENANCE lines; it holds 32.
- C2-2 (with F2-4, M2-2, M2-6). Applied:
  (1) The patterns are widened. clock_rx now covers an ISO date, H:MM and H:MM:SS, a compact 20YYMMDD[T]HHMM[SS]
      stamp, and a 16..-19.. number of 10 or more digits. dur_rx now covers a number with a unit (ms, msec, s, sec,
      m, min, h, hr and their long forms), XhYm and XmYs, an ISO PT duration, and the words elapsed, took, duration,
      wall, runtime and uptime.
  (2) A static AST scan of the driver's run path (its source outside _selftest) finds no time or datetime import and
      no clock or timer name (monotonic, perf_counter, strftime, strptime, ctime, asctime, time_ns, gmtime, localtime,
      process_time, utc_stamp, __import__, .time). It also finds no timing-field string, using the refuter's
      additions: 'seconds', 'hour_cap_ms_used', 'elapsed', 'stamp', 'sham_seconds' and 'contrast_seconds'. There are
      0 hits today.

  by the line forms.

  The package's lines are held to fixed forms, and the patterns cover the forms the reviewers listed. The canary is
  recorded as a candidate for the campaign wiring's review. The refuter's remaining misses are stated as limits:
  '2026/10/13', '13 Oct 2026', a hex epoch, '(in 0.12)', 'T+4.2' and a bare '[4.21]'.
- M2-3. Applied in the refuter's better form: stages._provenance_line is spied from the start of the wiring section
  to the line-form check. The PROVENANCE lines the wiring runs print must equal, in order and byte for byte, the lines
  the stage's own _append_provenance built. This kills r11c (a PROVENANCE line on the STOP path only), which the
  form check alone passed. r1e: 32 of 32.
- M2-4. Applied with the refuter's addition: m2_multi runs without the prior stub, so the joined REFUSED line also
  carries the real prior gate's PRIOR STAGE ABSENT.
- M2-5, M2-6. Applied as in r1d.
- M2-7. Applied as in r1d. The help phrase is matched after rejoining hyphen-wrapped words; the lens verified this at
  COLUMNS from 40 to 160. The refuter's wrap-proof alternative, asserting on the subparser action's help, is not used:
  main builds the parser locally.
r1e's self-test gives 795/795 under four conditions: HF_HUB_OFFLINE unset, HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1, PYTHONWARNINGS=always,
and TMPDIR under a '/7h/' segment.
Round 3's batch runs on r1e: 113 mutants and 2 baselines. It includes:
- the refuter's r02, r14b, r11b and r11c;
- Code's n12, the revision check skipped whenever a G-F0m-Qwen checkpoint is on disk.
A batch on r1d was stopped after 22 outputs, because r1e superseded it. Those outputs are kept aside, unused.

ROUND 3 -- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Round 3 is workflow wf_e40be2aa-398, a review of m_sessions.qual_r1e.py (sha256 e449c041...1eea). Three lenses
returned (fidelity, self-test, launch ops), each followed by one refuter. Code's round-3 batch also ran on r1e: 113
mutants and 2 baselines (r3mut). It is summarized in section 3 above.
- Baselines: 795/795 in both env modes.
- Every mutant was KILLED except the two equivalents declared in round 1 (q09, q11d).
Round 3's findings were applied in the patch scripts. The build is m_sessions.qual_r1f.py, which is the committed
driver. Its final batch, below, ran on it.

Self-test (S3): every one CONFIRMED
- S3-1 (major). Applied in the refuter's better form:
  - clock_rx uses digit lookarounds where a letter may abut the digits. It now covers the package's own
    resume.utc_stamp form, 2026-10-13T14:32:01Z, and resume._set_aside's 20261013T143201Z. r1d had dropped r1b's ISO
    arm, and its word-boundary arms missed these.
  - The compact arm ends at a 'Z' or a non-alphanumeric, so a hex token that begins with '20' and ten digits does not
    fire.
  - The epoch arm keeps \b.
  - A positive-control check asserts that the form check flags planted lines in those forms and a duration.
- S3-2 (= F3-2). Applied in the lens's form. stages.run_gate_stage and run_gid_pair are wrapped with a depth counter
  for the wiring section. A PROVENANCE line that stages._provenance_line builds outside a stage call is recorded as
  "built outside a stage call: ...", and fails the printed == built comparison. This kills:
  - f01 and s04 (the driver prints the package-built line at a STOP);
  - g01 and s03 (the driver calls append_provenance at a STOP).
  The fidelity refuter's alternative, a caller-frame test, is not needed: the stage-depth test covers g01. The check
  name now says what is tested.
- S3-3. Applied: the temp root, the repository and the interpreter path are replaced by '<root>' before both
  searches. A stamp appended after a root stays visible. In the final batch, a dated TMPDIR and a clone named
  '2026-10-12' each pass.

Record fidelity (F3): every one CONFIRMED
- F3-1. Applied: the comment's two clauses are restored. GATE and LICENCE print on no stub run (stages.py:512-513,
  :616-617). PATH and RE-FINISH print only where an end is pending, which no wiring run reaches. The step-14 entry
  words it the same way.
- F3-2. Applied (= S3-2).
- F3-3. Applied: the joined refusal is asserted on the single "REFUSED: G-F0m-Qwen (Q-F0):" line itself. This kills
  f02 and g02.
- F3-4 (= O3-3). Applied: the header's resume rule compares the DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines.
- F3-5. Applied in the refuter's better form. The header splits its rules into four paragraphs:
  - "While the stage runs";
  - "From v0.2's commit until G-F0m-Qwen's outputs are committed", which names the outputs' own commit as the
    exception and holds the driver-file and hub-cache rules;
  - "Exits and resumes";
  - "Until Q-F0's VERDICTS".
- F3-6. Applied: the run-path scan's comment states its limit. A time relayed from a package value is covered only by
  the line forms and the PROVENANCE checks.
- F3-7. Applied: the module docstring's --selftest list names stdout and stderr hygiene and the PROVENANCE lines'
  origin.

Launch procedure (O3): all applied in gen_header14.py
- O3-1 (blocker; CONFIRMED). The round-2 end-wait would have ended at once: nohup's PID execs the zsh -c wrapper,
  and caffeinate holds its assertions from a child of it. Applied in the refuter's better form: wait while kill -0
  holds and `ps -p PID -o command=` matches '[m]_sessions\.py qual'. The lens's probe (r3_ops/pidwait_probe.sh) is
  the record.

  EXIT lines are counted without printing, one per invocation.
- O3-3 (= F3-4). Applied.
- O3-4 (CONFIRMED). Applied: the generator takes the expected driver sha256 (the self-tested r1f blob) and asserts
  that the working tree and HEAD's blob equal it, and that it is not v0.2's eb6aa7cf.
- O3-5 (CONFIRMED). Applied:
  - the generator reads refs/main, lists the snapshot directories and derives the directory, asserting each;
  - it reads the dry precheck's output (dry14.py) and asserts its DRIVER and SNAPSHOT lines, and that the identity and
    the precheck returned no problems;
  - it reads the checklist itself (power, pgrep, disk, macOS). Only the audit session's state is passed in.

  `; echo "PID=$!"`.
- O3-7 (PLAUSIBLE). Applied: the generator prints the header, and Code checks it there. The raw is never opened with
  Read, Edit or Write from the header to the end.
- O3-8 (CONFIRMED; better). Applied:
  - valence_core/adapters/__init__.py and base.py are listed beside qwen_mlx.py;
  - the no-edit rule covers valence_core/**;
  - the seven clone closure hashes are asserted equal to G-ID-F0m's header, and qwen_mlx.py to lock §5.2's a3fc7b45...
- O3-9 (CONFIRMED). Applied: no add or commit of this raw, or of results/raw/ wholesale, before the end. The PI is
  told for the parallel session.
- O3-10 (CONFIRMED). Applied:

    typed command;
  - the raw path is asserted absolute and under results/raw.
